'@ function Get-OmegaLoadingLogo { # BUILD 2: decodifica una sola vez y cachea (Frozen = reutilizable seguro) if ($script:OmegaLoadingLogoCached) { return $script:OmegaLoadingLogoCached } if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($script:OmegaLoadingLogoB64)) { return $null } try { $bytes = [Convert]::FromBase64String($script:OmegaLoadingLogoB64) $ms = New-Object System.IO.MemoryStream(,$bytes) $bmp = New-Object System.Windows.Media.Imaging.BitmapImage $bmp.BeginInit() $bmp.StreamSource = $ms $bmp.CacheOption = [System.Windows.Media.Imaging.BitmapCacheOption]::OnLoad $bmp.EndInit() $bmp.Freeze() $script:OmegaLoadingLogoCached = $bmp return $bmp } catch { return $null } } function Get-OmegaEmbeddedLogo { param([string]$B64) if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($B64)) { return $null } try { $bytes = [Convert]::FromBase64String($B64) $ms = New-Object System.IO.MemoryStream(,$bytes) $bmp = New-Object System.Windows.Media.Imaging.BitmapImage $bmp.BeginInit() $bmp.StreamSource = $ms $bmp.CacheOption = [System.Windows.Media.Imaging.BitmapCacheOption]::OnLoad $bmp.EndInit() $bmp.Freeze() return $bmp } catch { return $null } } # V4.4.1 BUILD 2: ejecuta comandos de consola SIN ventana visible. # Necesario para compilar con ps12exe -noConsole sin destellos de consola # y para que la app no arrastre una terminal pegada. function Invoke-OmegaHiddenCmd { param( [string]$FilePath, [string[]]$ArgumentList = @(), [switch]$CaptureOutput, [switch]$Async, [int]$TimeoutSec = 60 ) $result = $null $p = $null try { if (-not [System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($FilePath)) { $probe = Join-Path ([Environment]::SystemDirectory) $FilePath if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $probe) { $FilePath = $probe } } $psi = New-Object System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo $psi.FileName = $FilePath if ($ArgumentList -and $ArgumentList.Count -gt 0) { $psi.Arguments = ($ArgumentList | ForEach-Object { if ("$_" -match '\s') { '"' + ("$_" -replace '"', '\"') + '"' } else { [string]$_ } }) -join ' ' } $psi.UseShellExecute = $false $psi.CreateNoWindow = $true # Un proceso async no redirige las tuberías para evitar que se bloquee si escribe mucho. $psi.RedirectStandardOutput = [bool]$CaptureOutput $psi.RedirectStandardError = [bool]$CaptureOutput $p = [System.Diagnostics.Process]::Start($psi) if ($Async -and -not $CaptureOutput) { # El llamador debe devolver este Process desde -Action para que la GUI lo sondee. return $p } if ($CaptureOutput) { $outTask = $p.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync() $errTask = $p.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync() } $limit = [Math]::Max(1, $TimeoutSec) $exited = $p.WaitForExit($limit * 1000) if (-not $exited) { try { $p.Kill() } catch { } try { $p.WaitForExit(2000) | Out-Null } catch { } Write-OmegaLog ("[TIMEOUT] {0} excedió {1} segundos; el proceso se terminó." -f $FilePath, $limit) } if ($CaptureOutput) { $stdout = ''; $stderr = '' try { $stdout = [string]$outTask.Result } catch { } try { $stderr = [string]$errTask.Result } catch { } $exitCode = $null try { if ($p.HasExited) { $exitCode = [int]$p.ExitCode } } catch { } $result = [pscustomobject]@{ Output = $stdout; Error = $stderr; ExitCode = $exitCode; TimedOut = (-not $exited) } } try { $p.Dispose() } catch { } } catch { Write-OmegaLog ("[CMD ERROR] {0}: {1}" -f $FilePath, $_.Exception.Message) if ($CaptureOutput) { $result = [pscustomobject]@{ Output = ''; Error = $_.Exception.Message; ExitCode = -1; TimedOut = $false } } try { if ($p) { $p.Dispose() } } catch { } } if ($CaptureOutput) { return $result } return $null } try { Add-Type -TypeDefinition @" using Asegurar la carga de WPF para poder usar MessageBox desde el inicio Add-Type -AssemblyName PresentationFramework -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue function Test-OmegaAdministrator { $identity = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent() $principal = New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal($identity) return $principal.IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator) } $script:OmegaMutex =$null $script:OmegaMutexOwned =$false # 2. Control de Instancia Única try { $script:OmegaMutex = New-Object System.Threading.Mutex($false, 'Local\OmegaSolverV44_SingleInstance') try { $script:OmegaMutexOwned = $script:OmegaMutex.WaitOne(0,$false) } catch [System.Threading.AbandonedMutexException] { $script:OmegaMutexOwned =$true } } catch { $script:OmegaMutexOwned =$false } if (-not $script:OmegaMutexOwned) {$msg = if (Get-Command Get-OmegaString -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) { Get-OmegaString 'SingleInstance_Message' } else { "OmegaSolver ya se encuentra en ejecucion." } $title = if (Get-Command Get-OmegaString -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) { Get-OmegaString 'SingleInstance_Title' } else { "OmegaSolver V4.4.1" } [System.Windows.MessageBox]::Show($msg,$title, 'OK', 'Warning') | Out-Null if ($script:OmegaMutex) { try {$script:OmegaMutex.Dispose() } catch { } } exit 0 } # 3. Verificación de Permisos de Administrador y Reejecución (Elevación) if (-not (Test-OmegaAdministrator)) { try { $scriptPath =$MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path if (-not $scriptPath) {$saveMsg = if (Get-Command Get-OmegaString -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) { Get-OmegaString 'Save_As_Ps1' } else { "Por favor, ejecute el script desde un archivo .ps1 guardado." } [System.Windows.MessageBox]::Show($saveMsg, "OmegaSolver V4.4.1", 'OK', 'Warning') | Out-Null if ($script:OmegaMutexOwned) { try {$script:OmegaMutex.ReleaseMutex() } catch { } } if ($script:OmegaMutex) { try {$script:OmegaMutex.Dispose() } catch { } } exit 1 } $hostExe =$null try { $hostExe = (Get-Process -Id$PID -ErrorAction Stop).Path } catch { } if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($hostExe) -or -not (Test-Path$hostExe)) { $hostExe = Join-Path$PSHOME 'powershell.exe' if (-not (Test-Path $hostExe)) {$hostExe = 'powershell.exe' } } # Liberar el Mutex antes de lanzar el nuevo proceso elevado if ($script:OmegaMutexOwned) { try {$script:OmegaMutex.ReleaseMutex() } catch { } } if ($script:OmegaMutex) { try {$script:OmegaMutex.Dispose() } catch { } } $script:OmegaMutexOwned =$false # SINTAXIS CORREGIDA: Escape con acento grave (`"$scriptPath`") para soportar rutas con espacios Start-Process $hostExe -Verb RunAs -ArgumentList @('-NoProfile', '-ExecutionPolicy', 'Bypass', '-File', "`"$scriptPath`"") | Out-Null exit 0 } catch { $adminMsg = if (Get-Command Get-OmegaString -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) { Get-OmegaString 'Admin_Required' } else { "Se requieren permisos de Administrador para ejecutar OmegaSolver." } [System.Windows.MessageBox]::Show($adminMsg, "OmegaSolver V4.4.1", 'OK', 'Warning') | Out-Null if ($script:OmegaMutexOwned) { try {$script:OmegaMutex.ReleaseMutex() } catch { } } if ($script:OmegaMutex) { try {$script:OmegaMutex.Dispose() } catch { } } exit 1 } } # ---------- 4. XAML PRINCIPAL ------------------------------------------------ [xml]$xaml = @"
